The **autonomy** question (1) remains the most open: the agents executed an eight-wide backend fan-out and an eight-agent frontend wave flawlessly, but the human still confirmed the slate, made every merge call, caught the home-grid count discrepancy, and steered the serial pipeline — and the loop did not *notice* the central-registration friction or the `fe-tip` leak until they manifested (though, once manifest, both were contained and filed agent-first). On **cost convergence** (3) the evidence is mixed: the build delivered 8 calculators in a single session with green CI throughout, but it also surfaced a fresh layer of duct tape — a serial merge pipeline forced by frontend central registration (#107), a worktree-isolation leak (#98), a non-deterministic screenshot gate (#87), and a foreman-teardown standup failure (#113) — all *filed for agent-def fixes* rather than absorbed, so whether the toil shrinks is a question the next iteration's regeneration sweep will test. The throughline across 76 chapters is unchanged and strengthening: the most consistently-converging variable is the operator's ability to push intent — math-correctness, spacing, *taste*, and process — into durable, regenerable artifacts, kept honest by encoding each lesson at the layer that governs it. What this iteration proved is that **the loop's taste reach survives a clean, frozen-agent-set A/B**; what it left open is **how much of the build the system can drive without a human catching the failures it doesn't notice**. **Iteration-0005 — the first regen-only, frontend-only iteration — adds the next layer of evidence (Chapters 86–90).** On **reach/autonomy** (1), the strongest positive datum yet appeared: handed only the *intent* "hover-capable JS charts via importmap," an agent **installed and vendored the chart libraries itself** and navigated a real packaging snag (chart.js split-chunks 404ing under importmap) unprompted — the loop extended into *toolchain* decisions, not just markup. But the same sweep produced eight *correct-but-inconsistent* implementations of one convention (a shared `.stat-grid` component vs. inlined utilities at five min-widths), so the loop converged on the *what* and not the *form* (→ harvest #143) — the autonomy is real but not yet uniform. On **controlled generality** (2), 0005 is itself the cleanest layer-scoped propagation to date: a frontend harvest pushed across the whole catalog from spec, with the operator reporting *"no notes"* on the live result. And the **process loop** matured visibly: an ambiguous, repeatedly-tripped-over part of *how iterations are delivered* was named by the operator and codified into a six-phase lifecycle (Chapter 84); a recurring agent-death failure was investigated to its real platform root cause (correcting the assistant's own hypothesis) and fixed at the agent layer (Chapter 89); and the visual gate caught a class of bug — a chart that renders blank while passing every assertion — that **no other gate in the pipeline could**, then encoded the catch as a mechanical pixel-test (Chapter 88). The throughline holds: the most consistently-converging variable remains the operator's ability to push intent — now including the *delivery process itself* — into durable, regenerable artifacts, kept honest by encoding each lesson at the layer that governs it. What iteration-0005 proved is that **a harvest can be propagated across the whole catalog by a single layer-scoped sweep, and the agents can reach into the toolchain to honor a design intent**; what stays open is **whether that reach becomes *uniform* (the stat-grid divergence says not yet) and how much the system catches on its own (the visual gate caught the chart bugs the agents and their tests did not).**

## Artifact ledger

**Agents:** `project-manager-agent` (Opus, `color: blue`; tracking/docs/Issues — *renamed from `pm` in Chapter 36, PR #26 `a14c8c6`*; its iteration definition now carries the full regenerate-from-spec sweep, Chapter 35), `ci-monitor` (Sonnet, `color: green`; report-only CI watcher), `historian` (Opus, `color: purple`; writes only `JOURNEY.md`, background fire-and-forget, anchor-driven), `ci-monitor` (Sonnet, `color: green`; report-only CI watcher — **made retries-only in Chapter 89, PR #142 `22478ad`**: on a transient/inconclusive result it retries rather than blocking unbounded or giving up; the bounded-poll variant first built was dropped per operator direction "always use ci-monitor now that we've introduced retries"; the genuine *abrupt* API death stays orchestrator-re-dispatch territory), `frontend` (Opus, `color: cyan`; owns `app/views`/`app/helpers`/`app/assets` theme+tokens/Stimulus, reads `DESIGN.md` first, codes to BLEND + the JSON envelope, barred from math/models/controllers/migrations/routes; built & exercised in Chapters 21–25), **`dependabot-agent` (Sonnet, `color: yellow`; Bash + WebFetch, report-only dependency-PR triage — classifies bump + blast radius, reads changelogs, emits GREEN-LANE vs NEEDS-HUMAN; human-gated merge policy; its *one* write-action is `@dependabot rebase` when a branch is behind `main`; built/dogfooded/fanned-out in Chapters 28–31)**, **`backend` (Opus, `color: orange`; tools `Read, Write, Edit, Bash` — no web; *everything server-side of the route* — calculator math in `app/calculators/` plus routes, controllers, models, migrations/`db`, initializers, `lib`, jobs, rake tasks; regenerates calculators *from spec* — the `spec:v1` `engineering` issue body, parses `ARCHITECTURE.md §3.2`, never inventing reference values; creates its own worktree per task via `git worktree add`; barred from client-side-of-the-route code — ERB/CSS/JS/view helpers; written in Chapters 37–39, **merged to `main` via PR #28 (`75a0c8e`, Closes #6, Chapter 40); not yet run on a real calculator**)**, **`database-agent` (Opus, `color: pink`; tools `Bash, Read` — *read-only DB inspector*, no worktree (report-only, like `ci-monitor`/`dependabot-agent`); runs a live `information_schema` census on launch + reconciles it against a hand-written enrichment overlay so undocumented schema surfaces as explicit "negative context"; answers "what's the state of my local test run?" — test-run state, per-calculator/attribution/kept-vs-discarded counts, mode discovery *from the data*, RBAC; never writes data, migrations, app code, or agent defs; spec Chapter 56, **built + merged via PR #47 (`Closes #36`, Chapter 57)**, validated by a live read-only smoke test that proved 13 → 13 rows unchanged)**. Each agent carries a unique mnemonic `color:` frontmatter field (Chapter 33; PR #23 merged `13638ad`). *All seven agents now built.*
**North-star docs:** `JOURNEY.md` (this), `docs/PRODUCT.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, **`docs/DESIGN.md` (the BLEND design system: tokens, sharp-grotesk type w/ tabular numerals, component vocab, the arbitrary-value token guardrail, self-hosted Inter, **a relationship-based spacing scale — six tiers, *targets not minimums*, wrapping groups get a larger row-gap than column-gap, pills "never keypad-dense"; Chapter 50, PR #38 `08b83ef`**, **§4 "Mode picker" — always a native radio `<fieldset>` posting `inputs[mode]`; segmented control at N≤3/short labels, `.mode-option` option-list at N≥4/long, raw `<select>` reserved for N>~8 only; "Tab pills" scoped to horizontal page-level tab sets; Chapter 64, PR #64 `1e6f65e`**, **and the three iteration-0004-review UX conventions reconciled into §4 as the single source of truth — interactive *hover-capable JS charts* via importmap (lightweight-charts for line/time-series + a complementary lib for donut/breakdown, no-JS fallback retained, not colour-alone; supersedes the old "CSS-only for now"), the *responsive auto-fit stat grid* (min-width cards flowing ~4-wide → 2×2, tabular-nums that never clip at 6+ digits; refines the old "3-up row"), and the *date field with "Today" quick-fill* (explicit button on optional "blank means today" dates, never silent auto-prefill, required dates never auto-defaulted); Chapter 82, PR #125 `b9edba8` — `frontend.md` slimmed to *defer* to §4 + keep only its two agent-process residues, PR #123 `6df4c29`**)**, `CLAUDE.md` (**amended in Chapter 96, PR #152 `150830c`/`e764988` for the derived calculator registry: rule #3 reworked from "no central registration" to *"no hand-edited central registration"* + a new "The calculator registry" section — a `calculators` DB table derived by an idempotent ingest from `docs/INVENTORY.md`, reconciled against `Base.lookup`, CLI-refreshed, deprecate-never-delete, backend-builds-/-database-agent-operates; this deliberately departs from the original rule #3 per the operator's override of the assistant's code-registry recommendation, Chapter 94**).
**Frontend (agent-built, doc-driven; merged to `main`):** the first **BLEND reference implementation** — styled nav chrome, `home/index` (signed-in/out), `sessions/new` with the styled Chapter-14 flash (PR #8, `Closes #7`, `911c460`); **self-hosted Inter** via `@font-face` + Propshaft, with a `document.fonts.check` delivery test (PR #14, `Closes #11`, `d515f12`) — the agent's first real *iteration* (a defect fix). Integration tests per page assert render + states; Tailwind v4 `@theme` tokens compile into served utilities.
**Verification tooling (Chapters 23, 27):** Capybara/Selenium **full-page** screenshot system test driving every UI state → PNGs the session-side Claude `Read`s; the **`NO_COVERAGE`** split so the system pass doesn't trip the 100% gate; CI uploads `ui-screenshots` as an artifact (PR #10, `Closes #9`, `37b4de0`). **`bin/ci-screenshots <pr|run>`** pulls that artifact for the main-thread visual gate (PR #13, `Closes #12`, `dd06958`); later given a **sha256 manifest + `--baseline <dir>` diff** (UNCHANGED/CHANGED/NEW by hash) so every screenshot is accounted for deterministically — no sampling (PR #17, `6292fb2`/`a3389d3`). The hash-baseline approach is valid because CI renders are byte-stable run-to-run (proven empirically in Chapter 27).
**App foundation (first product code, subagent-built):** session auth (`User`/`Session`/`Current`, no email/reset/signup), RBAC (`RoleType` seeded `ADMIN`, `Role`, `User#admin?`), `Discardable` soft-delete concern, `Calculation` model + `calculation_logs` DB view (real schema in `db/structure.sql`), CLI rake tasks (`users:create`/`set_password`, `admins:grant`/`revoke`), SimpleCov 100% line+branch gate. Ran live end-to-end; **PR #5 (`Closes #4`) merged to `main`** (merge commit `a8b6f5d`), issue #4 closed, branch cleaned up. The placeholder views (`home/index`, `sessions/new`) are intentionally bare — the frontend agent's territory.
**Calculator scaffolding (hand-built plumbing, Chapter 39; merged to `main` via PR #28, `75a0c8e`):** `config/initializers/calculators.rb` (autoload root, defines the `Calculators::` namespace module first — a Zeitwerk requirement the `ARCHITECTURE` snippet omitted), `app/calculators/base.rb` (`Calculators::Base` — ActiveModel contract: `slug`, `lookup`, memoized `result`, `to_calculation`, abstract `compute`), `app/controllers/calculators_controller.rb` + `post "/calculators/:slug"` (one dynamic, registration-free route) — param handling hardened over the doc snippet to permit *exactly* `klass.attribute_names` (drops unknown keys instead of a 500; no `permit!`; `ARCHITECTURE.md §3` corrected to match). Tests at 100% line+branch: `Base` unit + controller integration (envelope 200/422/404, anonymous vs. attributed, recording, unknown-key regression) driven by a **test-only `Calculators::TestDouble`** — *no Percentage*, kept as the backend agent's uncontaminated first measurement.
**Page-serving seam (backend-agent-built, Chapter 47; merged to `main` via PR #34, `Closes #33`, `2635076`):** one dynamic `get "/calculators/:slug" => calculators#show` alongside the POST; `#create` gained a `respond_to` (`turbo_stream` + `json`) with the unchanged §4 envelope and the shared `record.save` *before* render (so Turbo and JSON persist identically). Render branches covered via a **test-only view path** (`test/support/views/calculators/` stub templates) — boundary held, no `app/views/` touched. 100% line+branch. Intentional **406 contract-narrowing**: a bare `POST` with no `Accept`/format is no longer answered.
**First calculator (the experiment's first real measurement — backend agent, Chapter 46; merged to `main` via PR #32, `Closes #30`, `781079b`):** `app/calculators/percentage.rb` — `Calculators::Percentage`, **5 modes** (`percent_of`, `what_percent`, `percent_of_what`, `difference`, `change`) via a `send(:"compute_#{mode}")` dispatch, `MODES`/`REQUIRED_INPUTS` constants, conditional per-mode validation, division-by-zero as 422 validation errors, `BigDecimal` throughout; 33 tests, 100% line+branch, rubocop+brakeman clean, **zero route edits** (rule #3). The compute endpoint `POST /calculators/percentage` is live. Three agent-self-surfaced flags carried into iteration-0001's log as raw feedback (`send`-dispatch pattern, numericality-vs-`:decimal` coercion, parallel-coverage quirk).
**First calculator page (frontend agent's first measured output, Chapter 48; merged to `main` via PR #35, `Closes #31`, `1408d0a`):** `app/views/calculators/percentage.html.erb` (tab-pill mode selector, per-mode inputs, increase/decrease segmented toggle for `change`, Turbo form), a bespoke `results/_percentage.html.erb` hero card + `_generic.html.erb` fallback, `create.turbo_stream.erb` + `_result.html.erb` (renders both success and 422 branches), `percentage_controller.js` (Stimulus mode-switching), `calculators_helper.rb` (display formatting + `calculator_error_messages` mapping keys → visible labels, Chapter 50), BLEND `@layer` component classes (`field-input`, `mode-pill`, `direction-pill`). 100% coverage, system/screenshot tests. the MMR miss), **#109/#110** (integer-coercion vs `only_integer`; non-numeric `:decimal` coercion → backend agent / `Base`), **#111** (SimpleCov-parallel undercount), **#112** (DESIGN financial-color + eyebrow conventions), **#113** (`engineering` — `bin/dev` foreman teardown in non-TTY shells → a headless standup `db:prepare` → `tailwindcss:build` once → `bin/rails server`). The close-out docs PRs (PM inventory/iteration-close + this `JOURNEY.md`) were re-dispatched after both died on a transient API socket error mid-run; merge authorized on clean CI. *Open:* **#15 (component extraction)** — deferred deliberately until premature-abstraction risk fades; **#61 (visual-gate baseline source)** — deferred; **#72 (meta agent-authoring agent)** — deferred; and the iteration-0004 finding queue **#86/#87/#93/#98/#107–#113** — filed, all targeting agent-def/contract/tooling fixes rather than calculator code. *Now-resolved follow-up:* growing the PM issue template to carry the full spec was completed as the `spec:v1` work (Chapter 41) + extended to per-layer issues (Chapter 45). **Post-iteration-0004 review + iteration delivery lifecycle (Chapters 81–84):** the operator's hands-on review of all 8 calculators produced three confirmed FE conventions (Today-button dates, responsive auto-fit stat grids, interactive JS charts) → codified into `DESIGN.md §4` (PR #125 `b9edba8`) with `frontend.md` slimmed to defer to it (PR #123 `6df4c29`, both merged `f8e42c7`); the **iteration delivery lifecycle** (Open→Build→Evaluate→Harvest→Journal→Close + layer-scoped/regen-only) codified into `CLAUDE.md` → PR #130 merged (`e878c66`/`124cde7`, Chapter 84); the PM expanded the closed iteration-0004 log with its harvest section and **opened iteration-0005** → PR #131 merged (`ab8be91`/`6fa212b`, pinned `e84b05a`, Chapter 86). Filed during this stretch: **#122** (scalable catalog UI for 191 calculators — argued to be the same work as #107), **#124** (per-calculator informational modal), **#127** (database-agent DB-persistence verification step), **#128** (`inventory.md` → `INVENTORY.md` rename), **#129** (agents Pages site — **built + deployed live via PR #132 `dd31674`**, lockstep-by-construction, the "codify in CLAUDE.md" item deliberately *not* taken per the operator), **#140** (the platform-level agent-death pattern — root cause recorded). **Iteration 0005 — the first regen-only, frontend-only iteration (Chapters 86–90):** opened pinned at `e84b05a`, an 8-wide FE-regen sweep propagating the iteration-0004 harvest; PRs **#133** Tip, **#134** Ohms, **#135** BMI, **#136** Percentage, **#137** Age (Today button + cutoff fix), **#138** Simple Interest, **#139** MMR, **#141** Amortization (interactive lightweight-charts + Chart.js donut, self-installed via importmap; two visual-gate-caught chart bugs fixed `f63938e`) — all merged (final regen state `250e9a4`); the visual gate caught the blank-donut + 1970-axis defects all markup tests passed. **ci-monitor retries-only** (the 0005 process harvest) → PR #142 merged (`849e176`/`22478ad`, Chapter 89). *Open from 0005's deferred harvest:* **#143** (consolidate the responsive stat grid into one BLEND component — the implementation-divergence finding), **#144** (codify the chart "did-it-paint" pixel-test + the importmap chart-lib packaging rule).
**Specs/plans:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-project-tracking-pm-agent-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-12-pm-agent.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-13-app-foundation.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-13-frontend-agent-design.md` (the BLEND spec; `5d92c25`), `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-13-frontend-agent.md` (the hybrid implementation plan; `e9eaad5`) — **all of this spec/plan is now built and merged** (Tailwind toolchain, `DESIGN.md`, `frontend.md`, the dogfood, the CLAUDE.md startup rewrite). **`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-14-database-agent-design.md`** (`270e9c2`) + **`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-14-database-agent.md`** (`5b1409b`) — the read-only DB inspector's design + implementation plan, both now **built and merged** as `database-agent` (PR #47, Chapter 57).
**Conventions added:** `CLAUDE.md` now carries a **Branching & PR workflow** (tenor-adapted), a rewritten **Starting the app** section (`bin/rails db:prepare && bin/dev`; **foreman from `Procfile.dev`: web + Tailwind `css` watcher** — the no-foreman property reversed in Chapter 21), the **"every feature ships tests"** mandate, a **per-PR visual gate** in CI/CD monitoring now requiring **every screenshot accounted for deterministically** (view it, or pass it by hash via `--baseline` and view only CHANGED/NEW — no sampling; Chapter 27), a now **exception-free CI-verification rule** — the main thread **MUST NOT run `bin/ci-watch`**; CI status is *always* verified by dispatching `ci-monitor` (no "quick glance" carve-out, since a judgment-call exception re-opens the loophole; the stale "until registered" caveat dropped — Chapter 59, PR #48), and a **"Dependency updates (Dependabot)"** section (dispatch `dependabot-agent`; human-gated policy; its one auto-action is `@dependabot rebase` when behind; **a major is NEEDS-HUMAN with no exceptions**, blast radius lives in the rationale never the lane decision — Chapters 28, 30, 34). `CLAUDE.md` **rule #1** now makes agent-first *self-enforcing via the regeneration sweep* — every iteration rebuilds all prior calculators from their specs with the latest agents, so a code-only fix is erased on the next sweep (Chapter 35). **Per-layer issues + PR-first for all of `main` (Chapter 45):** each calculator gets *both* a `backend` and a `frontend` issue (both carrying the full `spec:v1` body — the FE needs its own spec to regenerate from); `engineering` is now non-calculator infra/agent work; and **nothing pushes directly to `main`** — docs, iteration bookkeeping (PM), and `JOURNEY.md` (historian) all branch → commit → PR → human merge (`CLAUDE.md`, the PM def, `ARCHITECTURE.md §0/§3.2`, `historian.md`). **Inventory build-coverage (Chapter 60, PR #49):** the PM def's Inventory capability now specifies a derived **`Built (PRs)`** column (sourced from live GitHub each refresh, the standing rollup the on-demand model lacked), a **Source** column rendered as the `*` slug from `calculator.net/*.html` while still linking the full URL, and a **completed-first sort** — the inventory stays the *catalog*, the build-coverage view is *derived* (idempotency preserved), and the change was made agent-first (PM def edited first, then the PM dispatched to regenerate `inventory.md`). **Iteration close now mandates an inventory regen (Chapter 66, PR #69):** the PM def's "Close an iteration" procedure was tightened so closing an iteration *always* recomputes the `Built (PRs)` column and floats newly-completed calculators into the completed block — *"A close must never leave the inventory stale"* — because an iteration's built calculators and the inventory's build-coverage column are the same fact. **Inventory completed-block sort = BE PR# ascending (Chapter 66, issue #70, PR #68):** within the completed block, order by the backend completion PR number ascending (smallest first; pending block stays alphabetical by Category then Calculator; a completed row with no derivable BE PR# sorts last) — so Percentage #32 → Ohms Law #57 → BMI #58. **Visual-gate two-lane (Chapter 63, PR #59):** the CI/CD-monitoring gate is now explicitly two-lane and *never skipped* — a **UI-surface PR** (`app/views`/`helpers`/`assets`/`javascript`, a layout, **or the math of an existing built calculator**) → view every CHANGED/NEW shot against `DESIGN.md`; a **non-UI PR** → run the `--baseline` diff and confirm every shot `UNCHANGED` (proves rather than asserts; a surprise CHANGED/NEW shot stops the merge for a look) — closing the file-scoping shortcut the assistant had used, with the repo-specific caveat that editing a live calculator's math is UI-surface (its page renders backend results). **`DESIGN.md §4` "Mode picker" + `frontend.md` directive (Chapter 64, PR #64):** the mode/variant picker is always a native radio `<fieldset>` posting `inputs[mode]`; presentation keyed on count + label length (segmented at N≤3/short, `.mode-option` option-list at N≥4/long, raw `<select>` reserved for N>~8); "Tab pills" scoped to horizontal page-level tab sets; `frontend.md` points at §4 as the source of truth for pickers. **`DESIGN.md §3` carries a relationship-based spacing scale (Chapter 50):** six named tiers (affix/grouped/grouped-wrapping/stacked/section/layout), framed as *targets not minimums*, with two travelling rules — wrapping groups get a larger row-gap than column-gap, and tappable chips/pills must never read keypad-dense — replacing the vague "generous" + the timid `gap-2.5` floor that the agent had sat on; §4 also gained label-based validation-error guidance. `ARCHITECTURE.md` carries a **Spec** term (§0) and a **§3.1 build model** section (the full-sweep / regenerate-from-spec / parameterized fan-out model); `INDEX.md`'s iteration concept matches. The earlier "spec format TBD-until-#6" notes (§0/§3.1) were **resolved** in Chapter 41 by a new **§3.2** defining **`spec:v1`** — the calculator spec *is* its `engineering` issue body (`<!-- spec:v1 -->` marker + Category/Source/Complexity/Tags + Intent/Inputs/Outputs/Reference values/Notes); the PM authors it and owns the reference-value pins (calculator.net via WebFetch), the backend agent parses + reproduces them verbatim with no web access (PR #29, `614e18b`). `ARCHITECTURE.md §11` is the canonical testing taxonomy (backend + frontend layers, `NO_COVERAGE` rule, the deterministic screenshot rule). `CLAUDE.md` now carries a real **Worktrees** subsection (ported from `../tenor`, Chapter 39; **generalized in Chapter 54 / PR #43** to a *standing default for every writing agent* — build agents, PM, historian — with report-only agents `ci-monitor`/`dependabot-agent` carved out; the rule lives once and reaches every agent *by-reference* via the mandated read-CLAUDE.md-first Step 0, no per-agent duplication): both lanes — interactive sessions via `EnterWorktree`, **dispatched agents create their own via `git worktree add` under `.claude/worktrees/`** (gitignored), commit/push/PR from there but never merge or push to `main` — with removal at the post-merge cleanup. `historian.md` was made self-contained to match (worktree → commit → push → docs PR, dropping the old "commit-but-don't-push," Chapter 54). `CLAUDE.md` also carries an **"Orchestration — stay ahead of the airplane (main thread)"** section (Chapter 53, PR #40): the main thread is an orchestrator that proactively catches-and-kicks any unblocked work into the background, only the critical path gates — promoted from personal memory (memory copy deleted to prevent drift). That section was then extended with a **background-by-default rule (Chapter 67, PR #71)** — *default to backgrounding dispatched agents*; foreground (blocking) dispatch is the exception, reserved for when waiting is genuinely required (the canonical case: the end-of-session historian) — again graduated from a just-made personal memory, the copy deleted to prevent drift; a follow-up **foreground-carve-out sharpening** (idling on the critical path is *not itself* a reason to foreground — keep backgrounding even when the main thread just waits) was shipped as a single-sentence standalone edit (Chapter 68, PR #74, awaiting merge). `ARCHITECTURE.md §3`'s param snippet was corrected to permit exactly `klass.attribute_names` (no `permit!`; no 500 on unknown keys, Chapter 39). **Iteration delivery lifecycle (Chapter 84, PR #130):** `CLAUDE.md` gained a new **"Iteration delivery lifecycle"** section codifying the six ordered phases every iteration runs — **Open → Build (fan out) → Evaluate → Harvest → Journal → Close** — with the key correction that *evaluate → harvest* are named, owned phases placed **before** close (the ambiguity that bit iteration-0004, whose harvest happened after its nominal close); the harvest lands on `main` and becomes the agent set the next iteration pins at, the next iteration's regen sweep is the propagation mechanism, and the **new-build set may be empty (a regen-only iteration)**. A companion **layer-scoped regen** rule: a sweep rebuilds only the layer(s) whose agents/conventions changed (frontend-only / backend-only / both). Rule #1 was lightly reworded to match ("a sweep may be layer-scoped or even regen-only"). Codified directly in `CLAUDE.md` rather than a separate spec doc — a deliberate divergence from the brainstorming skill, *"a doc-about-a-doc would just create drift."* **The three iteration-0004-review UX conventions reconciled into `DESIGN.md §4` as the single source of truth (Chapter 82, PR #125), with `frontend.md` slimmed to defer to it (PR #123):** interactive hover-capable JS charts via importmap, the responsive auto-fit stat grid, and the date "Today" quick-fill — DESIGN.md owns the *what*, frontend.md keeps only its two agent-process residues (install the chart-lib importmap pin yourself; ship the worst-case-value stat-grid clipping test), closing the two-sources-that-drift risk the assistant caught in its own first attempt. **The agents Pages site auto-generation is the lockstep enforcement, deliberately NOT a `CLAUDE.md` rule (Chapter 85):** the site regenerates from `.claude/agents/*.md` on every push to `main`, so a written "update the site too" rule was judged redundant and declined by the operator — *"no need to codify, that's more perfect."* **`ci-monitor.md` is retries-only (Chapter 89, PR #142):** on a transient/inconclusive result the monitor retries rather than blocking unbounded or giving up; the genuine abrupt API death (a platform-level connection drop, root-caused on #140) stays orchestrator-re-dispatch territory; the main thread *always* uses `ci-monitor` now that retries exist (the direct `gh pr checks` glances used during the deaths are dropped).
**Tooling:** `bin/ci-watch`, `bin/ci-screenshots` (pulls the `ui-screenshots` CI artifact for the visual gate), `bin/snapshot-transcripts` (mirrors all session + subagent transcripts into the gitignored `conversations/` on every historian run), `bin/dev` (foreman: web + Tailwind watch), **`bin/serve-headless` (Chapter 78–79, #113, PR #116 — the non-TTY/agent standup: `db:prepare` → `tailwindcss:build` once (no watcher) → `exec bin/rails server`, `PORT`-overridable; the documented alternative to `bin/dev`, whose foreman teardown SIGTERMs Puma when the Tailwind watch exits in a non-interactive shell)**, **`bin/merge-cleanup <pr>` (Chapter 78–80, #86, PR #117 — the post-merge cleanup in the load-bearing order: `gh pr merge --merge` (no `--delete-branch`) → `git worktree remove` *before* branch deletion → local `git branch -D` **and** remote `git push origin --delete` as separate guarded steps → `git checkout main && git pull --ff-only && git worktree prune`; fixes the silent-remote-skip bug where `--delete-branch` aborts on the local delete while a worktree holds the branch; dogfooded on four real merges the same session it landed)**, **`bin/build-agents-site` (Chapter 85, #129, PR #132 — a standalone pure-stdlib Ruby generator: parses `.claude/agents/*.md` front-matter + body and emits a static "Agents of whizwheel" site, deriving each agent's purpose/model/tools + a writes-via-worktree-PR-vs-report-only role; deployed by `.github/workflows/agents-pages.yml` on every push to `main`, `_site/` gitignored so it's lockstep-with-the-defs by construction — live at `http://jayravaliya.com/whizwheel/`)**.

**Post-iteration quick-wins (the findings-queue drain, agent-built, Chapters 78–80; all merged, final `main` `013564b`):** five of the iteration-0004 findings triaged as quick wins and fanned out in parallel worktrees → PRs → merged via `bin/merge-cleanup`: **#113** → `bin/serve-headless` + a CLAUDE.md "Starting the app headlessly" subsection (PR #116); **#86** → `bin/merge-cleanup` + the rewritten "After merge" bullet (PR #117); **#98** → the worktree-isolation checklist (cd-into-worktree + `pwd` confirm + pre-commit self-check) added to all four writing-agent defs — `frontend`, `backend`, `project-manager-agent`, `historian` (PR #118); **#108** → `test/integration/home_catalog_test.rb`, a guard test deriving the page-backed calculator set from `app/views/calculators/*.html.erb` (not `Base.descendants`, to exclude `TestDouble`) and failing CI if any calculator's home-grid card is missing — closing the gap MMR slipped through (PR #119, `Closes #108`); **#112 (eyebrow half)** → all 8 home catalog-card eyebrows recolored from `text-faint` to the §1 `accent` green `#2f9e6f`, vision-confirmed pixel-exact (PR #120). The two close-out docs PRs that opened this stretch — **PM iteration-0004 close + inventory (+5/−0, regens advanced)** PR #114 (`748bcbb`) and **historian build-journal Chapters 69–76** PR #115 (`a053611`) — each merged only through `ci-monitor` + the non-UI baseline gate (0 NEW shots). *Carry-forward open findings:* **#87** (screenshot determinism), **#93** (`visual-qa-agent`), **#107** (frontend central-registration refactor — would subsume #108's class), **#109/#110** (integer/non-numeric coercion conventions), **#111** (SimpleCov parallel-merge undercount), the **financial semantic-color half of #112** (a DESIGN call); plus the still-deferred **#15** (component extraction), **#61** (visual-gate baseline source), **#72** (meta agent-authoring agent). The CLAUDE.md edits (headless standup, the `bin/merge-cleanup` ordering, the worktree-isolation checklist in the agent defs) are recorded under **Conventions added** / the agent ledger.
